[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': ' * trigger, which made the most ordinary request a student has ("open PT 90,\r\n * section 1, question 12") cost a click, a search field, and a decision about\r\n * what to type. LSAT Lab and 7Sage both put these three in the open, and so\r\n * does this: pick a test, pick a section, pick a question, and you are in it.\r\n *\r\n * It is a sibling of the finder, not a replacement. Addresses are what this\r\n * row does; searching question *text* is still the panel\'s job, and its trigger\r\n * sits at the end of the row.\r\n *\r\n * The third select always lists official question numbers. LR opens the chosen\r\n * question in the drill runner; RC opens its passage at the chosen question.\r\n */\r\nexport default function QuestionJump({\r\n  returnTo,\r\n  tone = "light",\r\n  bank,\r\n  withSearch = true,\r\n}: QuestionJumpProps) {\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { tests, loading, failed } = useFinderAddressIndex();\r\n\r\n  const [pt, setPt] = React.useState("");\r\n  const [section, setSection] = React.useState("");\r\n\r\n  const availableTests = React.useMemo(\r\n    () =>\r\n      bank\r\n        ? tests.filter((test) =>\r\n            test.sections.some((entry) =>\r\n              bank === "rc" ? entry.rc.length > 0 : entry.lr.length > 0,\r\n            ),\r\n          )\r\n        : tests,\r\n    [bank, tests],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const sections = React.useMemo<FinderAddressSection[]>(() => {\r\n    if (!pt) return [];\r\n    const all = availableTests.find((test) => test.pt === Number(pt))?.sections ?? [];\r\n    if (!bank) return all;\r\n    return all.filter((entry) => (bank === "rc" ? entry.rc.length > 0 : entry.lr.length > 0));\r\n  }, [availableTests, bank, pt]);\r\n\r\n  const activeSection = React.useMemo(\r\n    () => (section ? sections.find((s) => s.section === Number(section)) ?? null : null),\r\n    [section, sections],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const isRc = !!activeSection && activeSection.lr.length === 0 && activeSection.rc.length > 0;\r\n\r\n  const rcQuestions = React.useMemo(\r\n    () =>\r\n      (activeSection?.rc ?? []).flatMap((passage) =>\r\n        (passage.questions ?? []).map((question) => ({ ...question, passage })),\r\n      ),\r\n    [activeSection],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const thirdLabel = "Question number";\r\n\r\n  const openLr = (qnum: string) => {\r\n    const params = new URLSearchParams({\r\n      mode: "type-drill",\r\n      qids: `PT${pt}-S${section}-Q${qnum}`,\r\n      present: "smart",\r\n      returnTo,\r\n    });\r\n    navigate(`/drill?${params.toString()}`);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const openRc = (target: string) => {\r\n    const [passageId, questionId] = target.split(":");\r\n    const params = new URLSearchParams({ passage: passageId });\r\n    if (questionId) params.set("q", questionId);\r\n    navigate(`/rc/drill?${params.toString()}`);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const thirdDisabled = loading || !activeSection;\r\n  const thirdPlaceholder = !pt\r\n    ? "Pick a test"\r\n    : !section\r\n      ? "Pick a section"\r\n      : isRc\r\n        ? "Question"\r\n        : "Question";\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="qj" data-tone={tone}>\r\n      <div className="qj-row" data-search={withSearch ? (\r\n          <section\r\n            className="drill-observatory-analytics"\r\n            aria-label="Analytics workspace"\r\n            data-tour="mainhub-analytics"\r\n          >\r\n            <AnalyticsStage\r\n              tab={analyticsTab}\r\n              section={section}\r\n              onTabChange={setAnalyticsTab}\r\n            />\r\n          </section>\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': ' */\r\nexport function matchesLRDrillFilters(\r\n  question: LRQuestion,\r\n  filters: Readonly<LRDrillFilters>,\r\n  omitFacet?: LRDrillFilterFacet,\r\n): boolean {\r\n  if (omitFacet !== \'prepTests\' && filters.prepTests.length > 0 && !filters.prepTests.includes(question.pt)) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  if (omitFacet !== \'sections\' && filters.sections.length > 0 && !filters.sections.includes(question.section)) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    omitFacet !== \'questionTypes\' &&\r\n    filters.questionTypes.length > 0 &&\r\n    !filters.questionTypes.includes(question.qtype)\r\n  ) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    omitFacet !== \'reasoningTypes\' &&\r\n    !intersects(filters.reasoningTypes, question.reasoningType)\r\n  ) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    omitFacet !== \'questionFeatures\' &&\r\n    !intersects(filters.questionFeatures, question.questionFeatures)\r\n  ) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    omitFacet !== \'difficulties\' &&\r\n    filters.difficulties.length > 0 &&\r\n    !filters.difficulties.includes(question.difficulty)\r\n  ) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n  if (\r\n    filters.questionNumbers.length > 0 &&\r\n    !filters.questionNumbers.includes(question.qnum)\r\n  ) {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const query = normalizeSearchText(filters.searchQuery);\r\n  if (query && !getQuestionSearchText(question).includes(query)) return false;\r\n\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function filterLRQuestions(\r\n  questions: readonly LRQuestion[],\r\n  filters: Readonly<LRDrillFilters>,\r\n): LRQuestion[] {\r\n  return questions.filter((question) => matchesLRDrillFilters(question, filters));\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Counts each option after applying all active filters except the facet being\r\n * counted. This makes sibling options independently discoverable even when a\r\n * different option in the same facet is already selected.\r\n      navigate("/drill", { state: { mode, config, returnTo: drillReturnTo } });\r\n    },\r\n    [drillReturnTo, navigate],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /* RC\'s builder reads the passage index, not the LR question bank, so it\r\n     returns before `useQuestionBank`\'s loading gate — waiting on 5.5 MB of LR\r\n     questions to draw a reading drill would be a spinner for nothing. */\r\n  if (section === "rc") {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="drill-observatory-launcher relative overflow-hidden">\r\n        <div className="relative z-10">\r\n          <RcHubSurface embedded showHistory={false} onCancel={onCancel} />\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="drill-observatory-launcher relative overflow-hidden">\r\n      {/* No header here on purpose: QuestionPicker renders its own title and\r\n          Cancel control, so adding one would show two titles and two Cancels. */}\r\n      <div className="relative z-10">\r\n        {isLoading && (\r\n          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-14" role="status">\r\n            <Loader2 className="drill-observatory-ink-70 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" aria-hidden />\r\n            <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-60">\r\n              Loading questions…\r\n            </p>\r\n          </div>\r\n        )}\r\n\r\n        {!isLoading && error && (\r\n          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-14 text-center" role="alert">\r\n            <p className="drill-observatory-ink-85 text-sm font-medium">{error}</p>\r\n            <Button\r\n              type="button"\r\n              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}\r\n              className="drill-observatory-console-start !w-auto px-6"\r\n            >\r\n              Reload the question bank\r\n            </Button>\r\n          </div>\r\n        )}\r\n\r\n        {!isLoading && !error && manifest && (\r\n          <div className="drill-observatory-launcher-panel">\r\n            <QuestionPicker\r\n              manifest={manifest}\r\n              onStartDrill={(config) => start("type-drill", config)}\r\n              onCancel={onCancel}\r\n            />\r\n          </div>\r\n        )}\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Drill hub stage. Renders inside the persistent Classroom frame.\r\n */\r\nexport default function AcademyMainhub({\r\n  drillReturnTo = "/classroom",\r\n}: {\r\n  drillReturnTo?: string;\r\n}) {\r\n  const perms = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const { setActive: setOrbitalActive } = useOrbitalActive();\r\n  const { theme } = useTheme();\r\n  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();\r\n  const [smartDrillPulseActive, setSmartDrillPulseActive] = React.useState(true);\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  disabled,\r\n  onPick,\r\n  onGo,\r\n  canGo,\r\n}: {\r\n  lr: { pt: number; section: number; qnum: number }[];\r\n  rcPassages: { preptest: number; section: number }[];\r\n  rcQuestions: { preptest: number; section: number; qnum: number }[];\r\n  bank?: QuestionFinderBank;\r\n  disabled: boolean;\r\n  onPick: (query: string) => void;\r\n  onGo: () => void;\r\n  canGo: boolean;\r\n}) {\r\n  const [pt, setPt] = React.useState("");\r\n  const [section, setSection] = React.useState("");\r\n  /* The question keeps its choice like the other two. It used to reset itself\r\n     after every pick, which read as the selector forgetting what was asked\r\n     for — and left the row saying "PT 159 · Section 1 · Question" while the\r\n     field below it said Q5. */\r\n  const [qnum, setQnum] = React.useState("");\r\n\r\n  const pts = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    const all = new Set<number>();\r\n    if (bank !== "rc") for (const question of lr) all.add(question.pt);\r\n    if (bank !== "lr") for (const passage of rcPassages) all.add(passage.preptest);\r\n    return [...all].sort((a, b) => b - a);\r\n  }, [bank, lr, rcPassages]);\r\n\r\n  const sections = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!pt) return [] as number[];\r\n    const target = Number(pt);\r\n    const all = new Set<number>();\r\n    if (bank !== "rc") {\r\n      for (const question of lr) if (question.pt === target) all.add(question.section);\r\n    }\r\n    if (bank !== "lr") {\r\n      for (const passage of rcPassages) if (passage.preptest === target) all.add(passage.section);\r\n    }\r\n    return [...all].sort((a, b) => a - b);\r\n  }, [bank, lr, pt, rcPassages]);\r\n\r\n  const qnums = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!pt || !section) return [] as number[];\r\n    const all = new Set<number>();\r\n    if (bank !== "rc") {\r\n      for (const question of lr) {\r\n        if (question.pt === Number(pt) && question.section === Number(section)) {\r\n          all.add(question.qnum);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    if (bank !== "lr") {\r\n      for (const question of rcQuestions) {\r\n        if (question.preptest === Number(pt) && question.section === Number(section)) {\r\n          all.add(question.qnum);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    return [...all].sort((a, b) => a - b);\r\n  }, [bank, lr, pt, rcQuestions, section]);\r\n\r\n  const pick = (nextPt: string, nextSection: string, nextQnum: string) => {\r\n    if (!nextPt) return onPick("");\r\n    let query = `PT${nextPt}`;\r\n    if (nextSection) query += ` S${nextSection}`;\r\n    if (nextSection && nextQnum) query += ` Q${nextQnum}`;\r\n    onPick(query);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="qf-browse">\r\n      <label className="qf-field">\r\n        <span className="qf-field-label">Test</span>\r\n        <select\r\n          className="qf-select"\r\n          value={pt}\r\n          disabled={disabled}\r\n          onChange={(event) => {\r\n            setPt(event.target.value);\r\n            setSection("");\r\n            setQnum("");\r\n            pick(event.target.value, "", "");\r\n          }}\r\n        >\r\n          <option value="">{disabled ? "Loading…" : "PrepTest"}</option>\r\n          {pts.map((value) => (\r\n            <option key={value} value={value}>\r\n              PT {value}\r\n            </option>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </select>\r\n      </label>\r\n\r\n      <label className="qf-field">\r\n        <span className="qf-field-label">Section</span>\r\n        <select\r\n          className="qf-select"\r\n          value={section}\r\n          disabled={disabled || !pt}\r\n          onChange={(event) => {\r\n            setSection(event.target.value);\r\n            setQnum("");\r\n            pick(pt, event.target.value, "");\r\n          }}\r\n        >\r\n          <option value="">{pt ? "Section" : "Pick a test"}</option>\r\n          {sections.map((value) => (\r\n            <option key={value} value={value}>\r\n              Section {value}\r\n            </option>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </select>\r\n      </label>\r\n\r\n      <label className="qf-field">\r\n        <span className="qf-field-label">Question</span>\r\n        <select\r\n          className="qf-select"\r\n          value={qnum}\r\n          disabled={disabled || !section || qnums.length === 0}\r\n          onChange={(event) => {\r\n            setQnum(event.target.value);\r\n            pick(pt, section, event.target.value);\r\n          }}\r\n        >\r\n          <option value="">\r\n            {!section\r\n              ? "Pick a section"\r\n              : qnums.length === 0\r\n                ? "Passages only"\r\n                : "Question"}\r\n          </option>\r\n          {qnums.map((value) => (\r\n            <option key={value} value={value}>\r\n              Question {value}\r\n            </option>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </select>\r\n      </label>\r\n\r\n      <button\r\n        type="button"\r\n        className="qf-go"\r\n        onClick={onGo}\r\n        disabled={!canGo}\r\n        aria-label="Open the selected question"\r\n      >\r\n        <ArrowRight size={17} strokeWidth={2.2} aria-hidden />\r\n      </button>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\nrg: regex parse error:\n    (?:stage === \\)\n    ^\nerror: unclosed group\n'}]

## assistant

The top PrepTest, Section, and Question Number controls open one question immediately. The adjacent Find a question button opens a separate search dialog. Neither shares state with the drill builder.

Inside the builder, the typed PT/section/number field shares filter state with its PrepTests and Section controls. “Add exact questions” adds specific questions to a manual set. “Search question text” filters the builder’s results and generated pool. These three builder actions have distinct effects.

I made no edits.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '  const toggleQuestion = (qid: string) => {\r\n    setSelectedQids(prev => {\r\n      const next = new Set(prev);\r\n      if (next.has(qid)) {\r\n        next.delete(qid);\r\n      } else {\r\n        next.add(qid);\r\n      }\r\n      return next;\r\n    });\r\n  };\r\n  \r\n  // Add random questions\r\n  const addRandomQuestions = (count: number) => {\r\n    const available = filteredQuestions\r\n      .filter(q => !selectedQids.has(q.qid))\r\n      .map(q => q.qid);\r\n    \r\n    if (available.length === 0) {\r\n      if (filteredQuestions.length === 0) {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "No questions available",\r\n          description: "Try adjusting your filters to see more questions.",\r\n          variant: "destructive",\r\n        });\r\n      } else {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "All questions selected",\r\n          description: "All questions matching your filters are already selected.",\r\n          variant: "destructive",\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    \r\n    const toAdd = Math.min(count, available.length);\r\n    const randomQids = shuffleArray(available).slice(0, toAdd);\r\n    \r\n    setSelectedQids(prev => {\r\n      const next = new Set(prev);\r\n      randomQids.forEach(qid => next.add(qid));\r\n      return next;\r\n    });\r\n    \r\n    toast({\r\n      title: `Added ${randomQids.length} questions`,\r\n      description: `${randomQids.length} random questions added to your selection.`,\r\n    });\r\n  };\r\n  \r\n  const hasAnyFilter =\r\n    filters.prepTests.length > 0 ||\r\n    filters.questionTypes.length > 0 ||\r\n    filters.reasoningTypes.length > 0 ||\r\n    filters.questionFeatures.length > 0 ||\r\n    filters.sections.length > 0 ||\r\n    filters.difficulties.length > 0 ||\r\n    filters.searchQuery.trim() !== \'\' ||\r\n    filters.questionNumbers.length > 0;\r\n\r\n  // Materialize a fresh preview from the current filter pool.\r\n  // No filter is a valid pool — it means the whole bank — because every facet\r\n  // already treats an empty array as unrestricted. Refusing to generate from\r\n  // it was a dead end that made the panel promise a set it would not build.\r\n  const handleGeneratePreview = () => {\r\n    if (filteredQuestions.length === 0) {\r\n      toast({\r\n        title: "No questions match filters",\r\n        description: "Try relaxing your filters to find more questions.",\r\n        variant: "destructive",\r\n      });\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    const count = Math.min(setSize, filteredQuestions.length);\r\n    const sample = questionBank.shuffleQuestions(filteredQuestions).slice(0, count);\r\n    setPreviewedQids(sample.map((q) => q.qid));\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Convert the current preview into an editable selection (qids land in selectedQids)\r\n  const handleEditPreview = () => {\r\n    if (!previewedQids || previewedQids.length === 0) return;\r\n    setSelectedQids(new Set(previewedQids));\r\n    setPreviewedQids(null);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Launch the drill. Source-of-truth order:\r\n  //   1. selectedQids (manual picks)\r\n  //   2. previewedQids (generated preview)\r\n  //   3. otherwise: materialize now from filters (legacy fallback)\r\n  const handleLaunch = () => {\r\n    let questionsToUse: LRQuestion[] = [];\r\n\r\n    if (selectedQids.size === 0 && (!previewedQids || previewedQids.length === 0)) {\r\n      if (!hasAnyFilter) {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "Nothing to build a set from",\r\n          description: "Pick at least one filter or select questions from the table.",\r\n          variant: "destructive",\r\n        });\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (filteredQuestions.length === 0) {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "No questions match filters",\r\n          description: "Try relaxing your filters to find more questions.",\r\n          variant: "destructive",\r\n        });\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const count = Math.min(setSize, filteredQuestions.length);\r\n      questionsToUse = questionBank.shuffleQuestions(filteredQuestions).slice(0, count);\r\n    } else if (selectedQids.size === 0 && previewedQids && previewedQids.length > 0) {\r\n      questionsToUse = previewedQids\r\n        .map((qid) => questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n        .filter(Boolean) as LRQuestion[];\r\n      if (questionsToUse.length === 0) {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "Preview invalid",\r\n          description: "Generated questions could not be loaded. Try Generate again.",\r\n          variant: "destructive",\r\n        });\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n    } else {\r\n      // Use manually selected questions\r\n      questionsToUse = Array.from(selectedQids)\r\n        .map(qid => questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n        .filter(Boolean) as LRQuestion[];\r\n      \r\n      if (questionsToUse.length === 0) {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "Invalid selection",\r\n          description: "None of the selected questions could be found. Please try again.",\r\n          variant: "destructive",\r\n        });\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      \r\n      if (questionsToUse.length !== selectedQids.size) {\r\n        toast({\r\n          title: "Some questions unavailable",\r\n          description: `${selectedQids.size - questionsToUse.length} selected questions could not be found and were skipped.`,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    \r\n    const qtypes = Array.from(new Set(questionsToUse.map(q => q.qtype)));\r\n    const difficulties = Array.from(new Set(questionsToUse.map(q => q.difficulty)));\r\n    const pts = Array.from(new Set(questionsToUse.map(q => q.pt)));\r\n    \r\n    const config: TypeDrillConfig = {\r\n      qtypes,\r\n      difficulties,\r\n      pts,\r\n      count: questionsToUse.length,\r\n      selectedQids: questionsToUse.map(q => q.qid),\r\n      qfeatures: filters.questionFeatures.length > 0 ? [...filters.questionFeatures] : undefined,\r\n      reasoningTypes: filters.reasoningTypes.length > 0 ? [...filters.reasoningTypes] : undefined,\r\n      sections: filters.sections.length > 0 ? [...filters.sections] : undefined,\r\n      searchQuery: filters.searchQuery.trim() || undefined,\r\n      // No question-number metadata. `selectedQids` above already carries the\r\n      // resolved set, so passing it would duplicate state the runner does not\r\n      // read — and `TypeDrillConfig`\'s field is a scalar that cannot hold the\r\n      // list this builder can now express.\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    // Wave 3 / Item A — record exposures for the questions we\'re about to\r\n    // serve so Smart Drill and the other generators exclude them from the\r\n    // user\'s recent-500 window.\r\n    if (user?.id && config.selectedQids && config.selectedQids.length > 0) {\r\n      recordExposures(user.id, config.selectedQids).catch((e) =>\r\n        console.error(\'[QuestionPicker] recordExposures failed\', e),\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    onStartDrill(config);\r\n  };\r\n  \r\n  // Invalidate preview when filters, size, or selection change\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    setPreviewedQids(null);\r\n  }, [filters, setSize, selectedQids]);\r\n\r\n  // Reset reveal toggle whenever a different drawer opens\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    setRevealAnswer(false);\r\n  }, [drawerQid]);\r\n\r\n  // Clear selection\r\n  const clearSelection = () => {\r\n    setSelectedQids(new Set());\r\n    toast({\r\n      title: "Selection cleared",\r\n      description: "All selected questions have been removed.",\r\n    });\r\n  };\r\n  \r\n  // Live summary stats — selectedQids wins, then previewedQids, then filter pool\r\n  const summary = useMemo(() => {\r\n    let mode: \'selection\' | \'preview\' | \'pool\' = \'pool\';\r\n    let source: LRQuestion[];\r\n    if (selectedQids.size > 0) {\r\n      mode = \'selection\';\r\n      source = Array.from(selectedQids)\r\n        .map(qid => questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n        .filter(Boolean) as LRQuestion[];\r\n    } else if (previewedQids && previewedQids.length > 0) {\r\n      mode = \'preview\';\r\n      source = previewedQids\r\n        .map(qid => questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n        .filter(Boolean) as LRQuestion[];\r\n    } else {\r\n      source = filteredQuestions;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const qtypeCounts = new Map<string, number>();\r\n    const diffBucket = { easy: 0, med: 0, hard: 0 };\r\n    let minPT = Infinity;\r\n    let maxPT = -Infinity;\r\n    let attempted = 0;\r\n\r\n    for (const q of source) {\r\n      qtypeCounts.set(q.qtype, (qtypeCounts.get(q.qtype) || 0) + 1);\r\n      if (q.difficulty <= 2) diffBucket.easy++;\r\n      else if (q.difficulty === 3) diffBucket.med++;\r\n      else diffBucket.hard++;\r\n      if (q.pt < minPT) minPT = q.pt;\r\n      if (q.pt > maxPT) maxPT = q.pt;\r\n      if ((attemptCounts.get(q.qid) || 0) > 0) attempted++;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const qtypeMix = Array.from(qtypeCounts.entries())\r\n      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])\r\n      .slice(0, 4)\r\n      .map(([type, n]) => `${n} ${type}`)\r\n      .join(\' · \');\r\n\r\n    return {\r\n      total: source.length,\r\n      qtypeMix: qtypeMix || \'None\',\r\n      difficulty: `Easy ${diffBucket.easy} · Med ${diffBucket.med} · Hard ${diffBucket.hard}`,\r\n selectedQids.size);\r\n                    addRandomQuestions(count);\r\n                  }}\r\n                >\r\n                  <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />\r\n                  Add {Math.min(setSize, filteredQuestions.length - selectedQids.size)} Random\r\n                </Button>\r\n              )}\r\n\r\n              {selectedQids.size > 0 ? "analytics"\r\n      : stageParam === "type-drill"\r\n      ? stageParam\r\n      : "default";\r\n  const isLauncherStage = stage === "type-drill";\r\n\r\n  /* Sitting a section moved to the Testing Centre. Old links, bookmarks and\r\n     anything a student had open land there rather than silently falling back\r\n     to the hub\'s default stage with no explanation. */\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (stageParam === "full-section") navigate("/testing", { replace: true });\r\n  }, [stageParam, navigate]);\r\n  /* Build a Set needs the whole canvas: its picker is a dense two-column panel\r\n     that got its Set Summary column clipped inside the ~600px bay. */\r\n  const isWideLauncherStage = stage === "type-drill";\r\n  const analyticsTab = searchParams.get("tab") ?? "overview";\r\n\r\n  const can = React.useCallback(\r\n    (flag: PermissionFlag) => perms.loading || perms.is_admin || !!perms[flag],\r\n    [perms],\r\n  );\r\n  const canOpenAnalytics = can("has_analytics_access");\r\n  const canOpenJournal = can("has_journal_access");\r\n\r\n  /* Throwing the switch REPLACES the query rather than merging into it: an LR\r\n     launcher stage has no RC counterpart at the same key, so carrying `stage`\r\n     across would land a student on a panel that does not exist for the section\r\n     they just chose. Pushed, not replaced, so Back returns to the section they\r\n     came from.\r\n\r\n     The switch is unconditional. A half-built LR set is discarded without a\r\n     prompt — Joshua\'s call, 2026-08-26: a flip that stops to ask is not a\r\n     flip. */\r\n  const changeSection = React.useCallback(\r\n    (next: DrillSection) => {\r\n      rememberSection(next);\r\n      setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: next }, { replace: false });\r\n    },\r\n    [setSearchParams],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const openAnalytics = React.useCallback(\r\n    (tab: string = "overview") => {\r\n      setSearchParams(\r\n        { [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: "analytics", tab },\r\n        { replace: false },\r\n      );\r\n    },\r\n    [section, setSearchParams],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const setAnalyticsTab = React.useCallback(\r\n    (tab: string) => {\r\n      setSearchParams(\r\n        { [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: "analytics", tab },\r\n        { replace: true },\r\n      );\r\n    },\r\n    [section, setSearchParams],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const openLauncher = React.useCallback(\r\n    (kind: "type-drill") => {\r\n      setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: kind }, { replace: false });\r\n    },\r\n    [section, setSearchParams],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const closeStage = React.useCallback(() => {\r\n    setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section }, { replace: true });\r\n  }, [section, setSearchParams]);\r\n\r\n  /* Smart Reading Drill: one passage, untimed, answers after each passage —\r\n     RcHubSurface\'s own defaults, spelled in the URL so the builder mounts,\r\n     applies them, and starts itself. The student sees the builder for the\r\n     moment the passage index is loading and then lands in the drill; they\r\n     never have to press Start a second time. */\r\n  const startSmartRc = React.useCallback(() => {\r\n    rememberSection("rc");\r\n    setSearchParams(\r\n      {\r\n        [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: "rc",\r\n        stage: "type-drill",\r\n        count: "1",\r\n        timing: "untimed",\r\n        reveal: "per_passage",\r\n        start: "1",\r\n      },\r\n      { replace: false },\r\n    );\r\n  }, [setSearchParams]);\r\n\r\n  /* Escape leaves a stage. The stage is a room the hub opened, and every other\r\n     room in this app closes on Escape; the Cancel button stays the visible way\r\n     out. Deliberately narrow about when it fires:\r\n\r\n     - only when a stage is actually open, so it never swallows Escape on the\r\n       plain hub,\r\n     - never when something modal is open (the search panel, a Radix popover or\r\n       select). Those close themselves on Escape, and closing the stage out\r\n       from under them would take two rooms away for one keypress,\r\n     - never out of a field the student has typed into, where Escape is the\r\n       browser\'s own revert. An empty field is fine to escape from. */\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (stage === "default") return;\r\n    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {\r\n      if (event.key !== "Escape" || event.defaultPrevented) return;\r\n      if (document.querySelector(\'[role="dialog"], [data-radix-popper-content-wrapper]\')) return;\r\n      const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;\r\n      const tag = target?.tagName;\r\n      if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA" || target?.isContentEditable === true) {\r\n        const value = (target as HTMLInputElement).value;\r\n        if (value != null && value !== "") return;\r\n      }\r\n      closeStage();\r\n    };\r\n    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);\r\n    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);\r\n  }, [closeStage, stage]);\r\n\r\n'}]

## assistant

Current generation treats manual picks and filtered generation as separate modes. To guarantee selected questions and fill the remaining slots, compose one question ID list before preview, launch, and save. The main change points are [QuestionPicker.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-wt-drill-layout/src/components/drill/QuestionPicker.tsx:545) for preview and launch, its wide and narrow action panels, and its two save handlers.

The existing [QuestionPicker tests](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-wt-drill-layout/src/components/drill/QuestionPicker.test.tsx:1) have a deterministic seven-question bank suitable for testing mixed selections. I found no dedicated visual preview harness for this builder. No files were changed.
